```json
}
```

### Calculates the relative change in the sum of Laboratory.XCL_026 between November 1981 and December 1981. The formula is (Sum_Nov_1981 - Sum_Dec_1981) 

```json
{
  "kind": "metric",
  "formula": "CAST((SUM(CASE WHEN \"Laboratory\".\"XCL_015\" LIKE '1981-11-%' THEN \"Laboratory\".\"XCL_026\" ELSE 0 END) - SUM(CASE WHEN \"Laboratory\".\"XCL_015\" LIKE '1981-12-%' THEN \"Laboratory\".\"XCL_026\" ELSE 0 END)) AS REAL) / SUM(CASE WHEN \"Laboratory\".\"XCL_015\" LIKE '1981-12-%' THEN \"Laboratory\".\"XCL_026\" ELSE 0 END)",
  "grain": [],
  "population_scope": "SELECT CAST((SUM(CASE WHEN T2.\"XCL_015\" LIKE '1981-11-%' THEN T2.\"XCL_026\" ELSE 0 END) - SUM(CASE WHEN T2.\"XCL_015\" LIKE '1981-12-%' THEN T2.\"XCL_026\" ELSE 0 END)) AS REAL) / SUM(CASE WHEN T2.\"XCL_015\" LIKE '1981-12-%' THEN T2.\"XCL_026\" ELSE 0 END) FROM Patient AS T1 INNER JOIN Laboratory AS T2 ON T1.\"XCL_058\" = T2.\"XCL_014\" WHERE T1.\"XCL_060\" = '1959-02-18'",
  "upstream_scopes": [],
  "execution_contract": {
    "formula": "CAST((SUM(CASE WHEN \"r1\".\"XCL_015\" LIKE '1981-11-%' THEN \"r1\".\"XCL_026\" ELSE 0 END) - SUM(CASE WHEN \"r1\".\"XCL_015\" LIKE '1981-12-%' THEN \"r1\".\"XCL_026\" ELSE 0 END)) AS REAL) / SUM(CASE WHEN \"r1\".\"XCL_015\" LIKE '1981-12-%' THEN \"r1\".\"XCL_026\" ELSE 0 END)",
    "grain": [],
    "role_bindings": [
      {
        "role": "r0",
        "physical_table": "Patient"
      },
      {
        "role": "r1",
        "physical_table": "Laboratory"
      }
    ],
    "scope_sql": "SELECT CAST((SUM(CASE WHEN \"r1\".\"XCL_015\" LIKE '1981-11-%' THEN \"r1\".\"XCL_026\" ELSE 0 END) - SUM(CASE WHEN \"r1\".\"XCL_015\" LIKE '1981-12-%' THEN \"r1\".\"XCL_026\" ELSE 0 END)) AS REAL) / SUM(CASE WHEN \"r1\".\"XCL_015\" LIKE '1981-12-%' THEN \"r1\".\"XCL_026\" ELSE 0 END) AS \"value\" FROM Patient AS \"r0\" INNER JOIN Laboratory AS \"r1\" ON \"r0\".\"XCL_058\" = \"r1\".\"XCL_014\" WHERE \"r0\".\"XCL_060\" = '1959-02-18'",
    "requires_staged_execution": false
  },
  "source_history_ids": [
    "bird_dev:1185"
  ]
}
```
